Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the protozoa object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 206. The moduli space has dimension at most 930 with 4 connected components.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the protozoa decomposition. By minimality, the element a^206 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 930-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Statement: For any protozoa module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 999. The projective resolution has length at most 601 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

By induction on n, assuming the bound 999, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 601. QED.
